# 1003 new apps — 2026-11-20

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-19 19:50 UTC (20:50 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-20 19:25 UTC (20:25 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-18 19:50 UTC (20:50 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.

## Coverage
